The FSMC BCR registers (BCR1..4) have their address offset defined as 0xA0000000 + 8*(x-1) for bank x (x = 1..4). This means:
- BCR1 offset: 0xA0000000
- BCR2 offset: 0xA0000008
- BCR3 offset: 0xA0000010
- BCR4 offset: 0xA0000018
